    "statement": "Let $p(n)$ be the partition function. For $k>1$ and $d\\geq 0$, define\n\n$$\n\\Delta_k(n):=\\min\\left\\{\\lvert p(n)-m^k\\rvert:m\\in\\mathbb Z\\right\\},\n$$\n\n$$\nM_k(d):=\\max\\left\\{n:\\Delta_k(n)\\leq d\\right\\},\n$$\n\nand\n\n$$\nL(d):=\\max\\left\\{n:p(n)-1\\leq d\\right\\}.\n$$\n\n**Partition-theoretic stabilization conjecture.** For each non-negative integer $d$, there is a positive integer $N_d$ such that for $k\\geq N_d$,\n\n$$\nM_k(d)=L(d).\n$$\n\nHere $L(d)$ is described using the fact that $p(n)-1$ counts nonempty partitions of size at most $n$ without parts of size $1$.     "statement": "Let $G$ be a graph, let $chi(G)$ denote its chromatic number, and let $K_{chi(G)}$ be the complete graph on $chi(G)$ vertices. A **strong immersion** of a graph $H$ in $G$ consists of an injection from $V(H)$ to $V(G)$ and pairwise edge-disjoint paths representing the edges of $H$, whose internal vertices avoid the image of the injection. **Lescure–Meyniel conjecture.** Every graph $G$ contains a strong immersion of $K_{chi(G)}$.
